Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The interpolation variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 879 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 278 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the interpolation decomposition. By minimality, the element a^879 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 278-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
